{"timestamp":"2025-11-27T09:25:47.407516Z","level":"WARN","fields":{"message":"slow statement: execution time exceeded alert threshold","summary":"SELECT DISTINCT ON (s.id) …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT DISTINCT ON (s.id)\n                s.id as service_id,\n                s.data as service_data,\n                ac.product_id,\n                ac.characteristic_vector as product_vector,\n                ac.product_labels,\n                ac.location_vector,\n                ac.full_vector,\n                ac.chosen_location,\n                ac.usage_count,\n                u.id as user_id,\n                u.email as user_email,\n                -- ✅ Extractions produit (compatibilité client_service)\n                CASE\n                    WHEN (s.data->'produits'->>'prix') ~ '^[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$'\n                    THEN (s.data->'produits'->>'prix')::FLOAT\n                    ELSE NULL\n                END as prix,\n                s.data->'produits'->>'devise' as devise,\n                COALESCE((s.data->'produits'->>'has_variant')::BOOLEAN, FALSE) as has_variant,\n                s.data->'produits'->>'variant_dimension' as variant_dimension,\n                -- Calcul distance GPS\n                (\n                    CASE \n                        WHEN s.gps IS NOT NULL \n                             AND s.gps != ''\n                             AND s.gps ~ '^-?[0-9]+(\\\\.[0-9]+)?,-?[0-9]+(\\\\.[0-9]+)?$' THEN\n                            ST_Distance(\n                                ST_MakePoint($2, $3)::geography,\n                                ST_MakePoint(\n                                    CAST(SPLIT_PART(s.gps, ',', 1) AS DOUBLE PRECISION),\n                                    CAST(SPLIT_PART(s.gps, ',', 2) AS DOUBLE PRECISION)\n                                )::geography\n                            ) / 1000.0\n                        ELSE NULL\n                    END\n                ) as distance_km,\n                -- Score de pertinence basé sur correspondance vecteur\n                (\n                    -- Score de base : combien d'éléments du vecteur recherché matchent\n                    (\n                        SELECT COUNT(*)::REAL * 20.0\n                        FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                    ) +\n                    -- Bonus correspondances partielles\n                    (\n                        SELECT COUNT(*)::REAL * 10.0\n                        FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) LIKE '%' || LOWER(search_val) || '%'\n                        )\n                    ) +\n                    -- BOOST popularité (usage_count)\n                    (ac.usage_count::REAL * 2.0) +\n                    -- BONUS lieu exact\n                    CASE \n                        WHEN ac.chosen_location IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(ac.chosen_location) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                        THEN 50.0\n                        WHEN EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val, unnest(ac.location_vector) AS loc_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(loc_val) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                        THEN 35.0\n                        ELSE 0.0\n                    END\n                ) as relevance_score\n            FROM autocomplete_characteristics ac\n            INNER JOIN services s ON s.id = ac.service_id\n            INNER JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id\n            WHERE \n                ac.is_real_product = TRUE\n                AND s.is_active = TRUE\n                AND ac.identifiant_base = 'produits'\n                AND (\n                    -- Au moins UN élément du vecteur recherché doit matcher\n                    EXISTS (\n                        SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) LIKE '%' || LOWER(search_val) || '%'\n                        )\n                    )\n                )\n            ORDER BY s.id, relevance_score DESC, distance_km ASC NULLS LAST\n            LIMIT $4\n            \n","rows_affected":3,"rows_returned":3,"elapsed":"1.497973575s","elapsed_secs":1.497973575,"slow_threshold":"1s"},"target":"sqlx::query"}

{"timestamp":"2025-11-27T09:25:51.106225Z","level":"WARN","fields":{"message":"slow statement: execution time exceeded alert threshold","summary":"SELECT DISTINCT ON (s.id) …","db.statement":"\n\n\n            SELECT DISTINCT ON (s.id)\n                s.id as service_id,\n                s.data as service_data,\n                ac.product_id,\n                ac.characteristic_vector as product_vector,\n                ac.product_labels,\n                ac.location_vector,\n                ac.full_vector,\n                ac.chosen_location,\n                ac.usage_count,\n                u.id as user_id,\n                u.email as user_email,\n                -- ✅ Extractions produit (compatibilité client_service)\n                CASE\n                    WHEN (s.data->'produits'->>'prix') ~ '^[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$'\n                    THEN (s.data->'produits'->>'prix')::FLOAT\n                    ELSE NULL\n                END as prix,\n                s.data->'produits'->>'devise' as devise,\n                COALESCE((s.data->'produits'->>'has_variant')::BOOLEAN, FALSE) as has_variant,\n                s.data->'produits'->>'variant_dimension' as variant_dimension,\n                -- Calcul distance GPS\n                (\n                    CASE \n                        WHEN s.gps IS NOT NULL \n                             AND s.gps != ''\n                             AND s.gps ~ '^-?[0-9]+(\\\\.[0-9]+)?,-?[0-9]+(\\\\.[0-9]+)?$' THEN\n                            ST_Distance(\n                                ST_MakePoint($2, $3)::geography,\n                                ST_MakePoint(\n                                    CAST(SPLIT_PART(s.gps, ',', 1) AS DOUBLE PRECISION),\n                                    CAST(SPLIT_PART(s.gps, ',', 2) AS DOUBLE PRECISION)\n                                )::geography\n                            ) / 1000.0\n                        ELSE NULL\n                    END\n                ) as distance_km,\n                -- Score de pertinence basé sur correspondance vecteur\n                (\n                    -- Score de base : combien d'éléments du vecteur recherché matchent\n                    (\n                        SELECT COUNT(*)::REAL * 20.0\n                        FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                    ) +\n                    -- Bonus correspondances partielles\n                    (\n                        SELECT COUNT(*)::REAL * 10.0\n                        FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) LIKE '%' || LOWER(search_val) || '%'\n                        )\n                    ) +\n                    -- BOOST popularité (usage_count)\n                    (ac.usage_count::REAL * 2.0) +\n                    -- BONUS lieu exact\n                    CASE \n                        WHEN ac.chosen_location IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(ac.chosen_location) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                        THEN 50.0\n                        WHEN EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val, unnest(ac.location_vector) AS loc_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(loc_val) = LOWER(search_val)\n                        )\n                        THEN 35.0\n                        ELSE 0.0\n                    END\n                ) as relevance_score\n            FROM autocomplete_characteristics ac\n            INNER JOIN services s ON s.id = ac.service_id\n            INNER JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id\n            WHERE \n                ac.is_real_product = TRUE\n                AND s.is_active = TRUE\n                AND ac.identifiant_base = 'produits'\n                AND (\n                    -- Au moins UN élément du vecteur recherché doit matcher\n                    EXISTS (\n                        SELECT 1 FROM unnest($1::TEXT[]) AS search_val\n                        WHERE EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.full_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE LOWER(vec_val) LIKE '%' || LOWER(search_val) || '%'\n                        )\n                    )\n                )\n            ORDER BY s.id, relevance_score DESC, distance_km ASC NULLS LAST\n            LIMIT $4\n            \n","rows_affected":3,"rows_returned":3,"elapsed":"2.904431856s","elapsed_secs":2.904431856,"slow_threshold":"1s"},"target":"sqlx::query"}

(PRIORITAIRE)\n                    (\n                        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(\n                            CASE \n                                WHEN product->>'nom' ILIKE '%' || word || '%' THEN 8.0\n                                WHEN product->>'categorie' ILIKE '%' || word || '%' THEN 6.0\n                                WHEN product->>'description' ILIKE '%' || word || '%' THEN 5.0\n                                ELSE 0.0\n                            END\n                        ), 0.0)\n                        FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                            CASE \n                                WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                WHEN END\n                        ) AS product,\n                        unnest(string_to_array($1, ' ')) AS word\n                    ) +\n                    -- Bonus pour correspondance de mots 3.0\n                        WHEN s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' \n                             AND s.data->'category'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                        THEN 2.0\n                        ELSE 0.0\n                    END +\n                    -- Bonus pour correspondances dans plusieurs champs (pertinence élevée)\n                    CASE \n correspondances uniquement dans la description (moins pertinent)\n                    CASE \n                        WHEN s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur' NOT ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%' \n                             AND s.data->'description'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                             AND s.data->'category'->>'valeur' NOT ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                        THEN correspondance exacte dans le titre (le plus important)\n                        WHEN s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                        THEN 2.0\n                        ELSE 0.0\n                    END\n                )::REAL as fulltext_score\n            FROM services s\n            WHERE s.is_active = true\n            AND (s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%décoration%' OR s.data->'description'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%décoration%' OR s.data->'category'->>'valeur' ILIKE '%décoration%' OR unaccent(s.data->'titre_service'->>'valeur') ILIKE '%decoration%' characteristic_vector (sans lieu)\n                    (\n                        CASE \n                            WHEN s.data->'produits'->'characteristic_vector' IS NOT NULL THEN\n 8.0\n                                    FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(s.data->'produits'->'characteristic_vector') AS vec_val\n                                    WHERE vec_val ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                                )\n                            ELSE 0.0\n                        END\n                    ) +\n                    -- ✅ NOUVEAU 2025-11-04: BOOST pour ~50% caractéristiques / 50% description\n                    (\n                        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(\n                            -- Score de base pour correspondance dans characteristic_vector (SANS lieu)\n                            8.0 *\n                            -- Recherche dans le vecteur caractéristiques UNIQUEMENT (produit, pas lieu)\n        ) *\n                            -- BOOST selon popularité CLIENT (usage_count)\n                            -- 1 fois = 1.0x, 5 fois = 1.5x, 10 fois = 2.0x, 20+ fois = 3.0x\n                            LEAST(3.0, 1.0 + (ac.usage_count::REAL / 10.0))\n                        ), 0.0)\n                        FROM autocomplete_characteristics ac\n                        WHERE ac.service_id = s.id\n                        AND ac.identifiant_base LIKE 'produit%'\n                        AND ac.is_real_product = TRUE  -- Seulement les VRAIS produits prestataires\n                        -- Recherche dans characteristic_vector UNIQUEMENT (pas lieu)\n                        AND EXISTS (\n                            SELECT 1 FROM unnest(ac.characteristic_vector) AS vec_val\n                            WHERE vec_val ILIKE '%' || $1 || '%'\n                        )\n                    ) +\n                    -- 🔥 NOUVEAU 2025-11-04: Bonus unaccent pour champs PRODUIT (PRIORITAIRE)\n                    (\n                        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(\n                            CASE \n                                WHEN unaccent(COALESCE(product->>'nom', '')) ILIKE '%' || unaccent($1) || '%' THEN 12.0\n                                WHEN unaccent(COALESCE(product->>'categorie', '')) ILIKE '%' || unaccent($1) || '%' THEN 10.0\n                                WHEN unaccent(COALESCE(product->>'description', '')) ILIKE '%' || unaccent($1) || '%' THEN 8.0\n                                ELSE 0.0\n                            END\n                        ), 0.0)\n                        FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                            CASE \n                                WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                                THEN s.data->'produits'\n                                WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits'->'valeur') = 'array'\n                                THEN s.data->'produits'->'valeur'\n                                ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                            END\n                        ) AS product\n                    ) +\n                    -- Bonus pour correspondances sans accents SERVICE ), 0.0)\n                        FROM jsonb_array_elements(\n                            CASE \n                                WHEN jsonb_typeof(s.data->'produits') = 'array' \n                        ELSE '[]'::jsonb\n                            END\n                        ) AS product,\n                        unnest(string_to_array($1, ' ')) AS word\n                    )
